Inspired by expert workflows for PET image quality enhancement, we propose an VLM- and LLM-driven multi-agent PET denoising framework that dynamically assesses image quality and lesion status, autonomously selects optimal denoising models and parameters, and enables closed-loop feedback with rollback mechanisms. Experiments were conducted on Siemens Biograph Vision Quadra PET/CT data with 1/20 and 1/50 low-dose settings. Individual module evaluations demonstrated the reliability of the agentic components, while the complete framework achieved higher PSNR and SSIM than UNet, GAN, and DDPM baselines at both dose levels. These preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of using a closed-loop multi-agent framework to adapt PET denoising strategies to different image conditions.

</details>


### [193] [From crown candidates to neighborhood screening: integrating optical GeoAI and spatial modeling for urban-canopy assessment in Davis, California](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13856)
*Mohammadreza Narimani,Shreyan Mitra,Parastoo Farajpoor*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个光学GeoAI工作流，利用2022年NAIP影像（0.6m RGB+NIR）对加州Davis市进行城市冠层制图。通过DeepForest生成树冠候选、NDVI阈值、非极大值抑制和SAM分割，最终识别出11,741个树冠候选，覆盖2.43 km²（占城市9.37%）。与USDA/CAL FIRE的LiDAR辅助产品对比，像素级一致率为87.8%，但仅代表参考冠层面积的34.2%。冠层与地表温度呈负相关，且约49%的候选位于道路15米内，为城市规划提供了可复现的筛查层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 及时的城市冠层信息对于连接遥感和热环境、流动性及社区规划至关重要。现有结构化的冠层产品（如LiDAR辅助产品）更新成本高，需要一种可复现、可更新的光学影像筛查方案来补充结构产品，同时保留数据来源和空间诊断信息以支持规划决策。

Method: 流程包括：使用DeepForest生成树冠候选边界框；应用NDVI阈值筛选、非极大值抑制去除重叠候选；利用box提示的Segment Anything Model (ViT-B)生成冠层锚定的冠层表面。分析基于25.92 km²的Census TIGER市政边界和100m网格，并与Landsat地表温度和道路数据进行关联分析。

Result: 工作流保留了11,741个候选树冠，绘制了2.43 km²冠层（占城市9.37%）。与2022年USDA/CAL FIRE LiDAR辅助产品相比，87.8%的冠层像素和97.4%的候选中心一致，但光学表面仅代表参考冠层面积的34.2%（IoU 0.288, Dice 0.448）。约49%候选位于道路15m内；冠层与Landsat地表温度呈负相关（Spearman rho=-0.293，控制建成概率后偏相关系数-0.370），空间滞后模型证实了清晰的邻域结构。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可复现、可更新的冠层筛查层，能够补充结构化冠层产品和市政清单，同时保留假设、数据来源和空间诊断信息以供规划解释，是一种实用的城市冠层监测补充工具。

Abstract: Timely urban-canopy information is essential for linking remote sensing with heat, mobility, and neighborhood planning. We developed an optical GeoAI workflow for Davis, California, using 2022 National Agriculture Imagery Program imagery (0.6 m RGB+NIR). DeepForest generated crown candidates; an NDVI threshold, non-maximum suppression, and box-prompted Segment Anything Model (ViT-B) produced a crown-anchored canopy surface. Analyses used the 25.92 km2 Census TIGER municipal boundary and a 100 m grid. The workflow retained 11,741 candidate crowns and mapped 2.43 km2 of canopy (9.37% of the city). On the identical extent, 87.8% of mapped canopy pixels and 97.4% of candidate centers agreed with the 2022 USDA/CAL FIRE LiDAR-assisted canopy product; the optical surface represented 34.2% of the reference canopy area (IoU 0.288; Dice 0.448). Approximately 49% of candidates occurred within 15 m of a road. Canopy was inversely associated with Landsat land-surface temperature (Spearman rho = -0.293; partial rho = -0.370 controlling for built probability), and spatial-lag modeling confirmed clear neighborhood structure. Two transparent attention surfaces combined canopy need with thermal and contextual indicators. The framework provides a reproducible, updateable screening layer that complements structural canopy products and municipal inventories while retaining assumptions, data provenance, and spatial diagnostics for planning interpretation.

</details>


### [194] [Practical Lossless Volumetric Medical Image Compression via Tri-plane Context Tree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13897)
*Yuanchao Bai,Yifan Zhao,Kai Wang,Yuanbo Du,Jie Cheng,Teng Fang,Xianming Liu,Wen Gao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三平面上下文树（TCT）的无损体积医学图像压缩方法，无需深度神经网络或外部训练数据即可达到与DNN方法相当的压缩性能，同时保持低计算成本和快速编码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积医学图像的无损压缩要求数据保真度，但传统压缩方法因刚性手工模型效率有限，而基于DNN的压缩方法虽有效但计算资源消耗大，难以在资源受限场景中部署。因此需要一种高性能且轻量级的压缩方案。

Method: 提出三平面上下文表示，将复杂3D上下文建模分解为三个正交平面上的高效2D建模；将该表示集成到上下文树框架中，构建自适应二叉树结构的TCT模型，每个树节点动态选择三平面预测器和上下文特征提取器；通过从输入体积中采样子集，以最小描述长度（MDL）为目标进行迭代构建和剪枝来学习模型；每个像素基于学到的TCT模型检索上下文、计算预测残差并进行熵编码。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法的压缩性能与近期基于DNN的方法相当，同时保持低计算成本和快速编码速度，具有较高的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地在无需DNN和外部训练数据的前提下实现了与DNN方法相当的压缩性能，兼顾了压缩效率和计算资源需求，适用于资源受限的实际临床与科研场景。

Abstract: Lossless compression of volumetric medical images is of paramount importance for clinical and research applications where data fidelity is essential. Traditional compression methods are often limited in efficiency due to rigid, handcrafted models. Conversely, deep neural network (DNN)-based compression methods, while effective, demand substantial computational resources, hindering deployment in resource-constrained settings. To address these challenges, we propose a novel tri-plane context tree (TCT)-based method for lossless volumetric medical image compression that delivers high performance without relying on DNNs or external training data. To exploit intra-slice and inter-slice redundancies, we introduce a compact tri-plane context representation that decomposes complex 3D context modeling into efficient 2D modeling on three orthogonal planes. By integrating this representation with a context tree framework, we develop an input-specific TCT model employing an adaptive binary tree structure. At each tree node, the model dynamically selects from a suite of tri-plane based predictors and contextual feature extractors, enabling data-adaptive context modeling tailored to local structural characteristics. Instead of offline training, we sample a subset of the input volume to learn the TCT model by optimizing the minimum description length (MDL) through iterative construction and pruning. With the learned TCT model, each pixel retrieves its corresponding context, computes the prediction residual using the predictor dictated by the context, and performs entropy encoding based on the associated histograms. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves compression performance on par with recent DNN-based methods on multiple datasets, while maintaining low computational cost and fast coding speeds, making it highly applicable in practice.

</details>


### [195] [Personalized Digital Semantic Communication for Image Transmission with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14260)
*Nan Li,Li Zhou,Haijun Wang,Jun Xiong,Haitao Zhao,Jibo Wei*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种个性化数字语义通信（PDSC）框架，结合视觉-语言模型（VLM）语义编码器和潜在扩散模型（LDM）语义解码器，实现面向接收者个性化偏好的带宽高效无线图像传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信方案大多与用户无关，忽略了接收者依赖的语义信息，无法满足不同用户的个性化需求。

Method: PDSC框架包含两个核心组件：VLM-based语义编码器从源图像和接收者历史交互中提取个性化语义令牌，经矢量量化编码为紧凑定长比特流以兼容数字传输；LDM-based语义解码器则基于恢复的语义令牌重建个性化图像。同时构建了容量受限的个性化语义率失真问题，并提出联合刻画源语义保真度和用户偏好对齐的语义失真度量。

Result: 实验表明，在带宽受限的无线传输条件下，PDSC相比现有的最先进语义通信基线（CDDM和MoS）在源语义一致性和个性化方面均取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将用户偏好注入语义编码过程并采用数字化的离散令牌传输机制，PDSC在保持源语义保真的同时实现了接收端个性化图像重建，验证了将VLMs和LDMs结合用于个性化语义通信的有效性。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SC) enables bandwidth-efficient wireless image transmission, but most existing SC schemes are user-agnostic and ignore receiver-dependent semantics. To address this issue, we propose a personalized digital semantic communication (PDSC) framework that integrates a vision-language model (VLM)-based semantic encoder with a latent diffusion model (LDM)-based semantic decoder. Specifically, the semantic encoder extracts source-aware personalized semantic tokens from both the source image and the receiver's historical interactions. These tokens are vector-quantized into discrete semantic indices and further encoded into a compact fixed-length bitstream, enabling compatibility with digital transmission. At the receiver, the semantic decoder reconstructs a personalized image conditioned on the recovered semantic tokens. Furthermore, we formulate a capacity-constrained personalized semantic rate-distortion problem and introduce a semantic distortion metric that jointly characterizes source-semantic fidelity and user-preference alignment. Experiments show that PDSC achieves superior source-semantic consistency and personalization over state-of-the-art SC baselines, including CDDM and MoS, under bandwidth-limited wireless transmission.

</details>


### [196] [A Subjective Study on a New Sharpness Informed Class of Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13989)
*Uditangshu Aurangabadkar,Vibhoothi Vibhoothi,Darren Ramsook,Anil Kokaram*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文针对DNN图像去模糊中感知损失函数忽略显式锐度优化的问题，提出了一种显式针对锐度的损失训练方案，并引入均匀锐度增量数据集和DMOS评分，同时提出新的SI-IQA指标（SI-PSNR）来惩罚过度锐化，实验表明锐度感知复合损失在67%的对比中被用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN去模糊架构中的感知损失函数虽然能改善整体图像质量，但很少有方法显式地针对图像锐度进行优化，导致恢复图像的锐度水平不受控，且缺乏对过度锐化问题的合理评估手段。

Method: 采用四协议方法进行主观研究，探索偏好锐度水平及其对图像质量的影响；构建具有均匀锐度增量的新数据集并获取DMOS；提出一类新颖的锐度感知IQA指标（SI-PSNR）以正确惩罚过度锐化；训练采用锐度感知复合损失的模型与未显式针对锐度的模型进行对比。

Result: 新提出的SI-PSNR指标在IQA基准数据集上的相关性统计优于所有其他PSNR变体；使用锐度感知复合损失的模型在67%的二值化对比中被用户偏好，优于未显式针对锐度的损失。

Conclusion: 显式针对锐度的损失函数能够显著提升去模糊图像的用户偏好度，同时提出的SI-PSNR指标为过度锐化提供了更合理的评价方式，弥补了现有感知损失在锐度方面考虑的不足。

Abstract: Perceptual loss functions in Deep Neural Network (DNN) deblurring architectures improve the overall quality of restored images. However, few focus on explicitly targeting sharpness in the restorations. We conduct a subjective study of models trained with and without losses which explicitly target sharpness using a four-protocol approach, exploring preferred sharpness levels and effects on image quality. We introduce a novel dataset of images with uniform sharpness increments along with Difference Mean Opinion Scores (DMOS). Additionally, we propose a novel class of Sharpness Informed (SI) Image Quality Assessment (IQA) metrics which properly penalize over-sharpening. Our new SI-PSNR metric outperforms all other PSNR variants in terms of correlation statistics on IQA benchmarking datasets. We show that, on average, images restored using a sharpness-aware composite loss are preferred in 67% of binarized comparisons, as opposed to losses that do not explicitly target sharpness.

</details>


### [197] [UMPIRE-Net: Unrolled Magnitude-Phase Regularization Network for Accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14422)
*Mahdi Saberi,Toygan Kiliç,Mehmet Akçakaya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出UMPIRE-Net，一种物理驱动的深度学习MRI重建方法，通过将幅度和相位作为独立分量分别进行学习正则化，并结合新的数据保真项，在部分傅里叶加速MRI重建中优于传统的复数值方法，获得更清晰的图像和更少的伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PD-DL方法直接重建复数值图像，隐式耦合了幅度和相位信息，这种耦合正则化在部分傅里叶成像等对相位建模敏感的场景中可能不够理想，因为亚采样k空间数据的恢复依赖于底层图像相位。

Method: 提出UMPIRE-Net，在算法展开框架中为幅度和相位分量分别引入独立的学习正则器，并设计了一种新的数据保真度公式来确保测量一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集和加速因子下，UMPIRE-Net相比传统复数值PD-DL基线方法，重建质量更优，图像更清晰且伪影更少。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离幅度与相位的正则化建模，可以减少对外部估计或预定义相位信息的依赖，从而提升部分傅里叶加速MRI的重建性能。

Abstract: MRI reconstruction from undersampled k-space measurements is an ill-posed inverse problem. Physics-driven deep learning (PD-DL) methods have shown strong performance for this task by combining the MRI forward model with learned image regularization within algorithm-unrolling frameworks. However, most existing PD-DL methods reconstruct complex-valued images directly, thereby implicitly coupling magnitude and phase within a single learned representation. This coupled regularization may be suboptimal in reconstruction settings where accurate phase modeling plays an important role, such as partial Fourier (PF) imaging, where recovery of the omitted asymmetric k-space measurements depends on the underlying image phase. In such scenarios, explicit modeling of magnitude and phase as separate components may reduce the reliance on externally estimated or predefined phase information. To this end, we propose UMPIRE-Net (Unrolled Magnitude-Phase In REgularization Network), a PD-DL method that introduces separate learned regularizers for magnitude and phase components, together with a novel data-fidelity formulation that enforces measurements consistency. We evaluate UMPIRE-Net for accelerated MRI with PF across different datasets and acceleration factors. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed method improves reconstruction quality compared with a conventional complex-valued PD-DL baseline, yielding sharper images and reduced artifacts. Code available at: https://github.com/MahdiSaberii/UMPIRE-Net

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Towards Scaling Qualitative Analysis of Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13594)
*Shiyi He*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QualiVision，一个交互式电子表格工作区设计探针，旨在通过整合视频、转录稿、编码流、初步报告、启发式可视化和AI支持，来支持视频定性分析的可扩展性，帮助研究者保留证据、比较解释并开展迭代反思性意义构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究规模扩大，定性视频分析难以扩展。现有方法在支持大规模视频数据的证据保留、解释比较和迭代反思方面存在不足，亟需新的工具来应对这一挑战。

Method: 该研究设计了一个名为QualiVision的设计探针，采用交互式电子表格支持的工作区方式，集成视频播放、转录文本、编码数据流、初步分析报告、启发式可视化以及AI辅助支持等多种功能模块。

Result: 论文尚未提供具体实验结果，主要描述了QualiVision作为设计探针的概念设计和工作区架构，旨在探索如何通过整合多种功能来改善视频定性分析的可扩展性体验。

Conclusion: QualiVision有望为视频定性分析提供一种新的交互范式，使研究者能够在分析不断演进的过程中保留证据、比较不同解释并开展持续的反思性意义构建。

Abstract: Scaling qualitative video analysis is difficult as studies grow. This paper presents QualiVision, a design probe examining how an interactive, spreadsheet-backed workspace can support video-based qualitative analysis. By integrating video, transcripts, coding streams, preliminary reports, heuristic visualization and AI support, QualiVision aims to help researchers preserve evidence, compare interpretations, and conduct iterative, reflexive sensemaking as their analysis evolves.

</details>


### [199] [Omni-LiveAvatar: Minute-Level Real-Time Streaming Joint Audio-Visual Avatar Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13602)
*Lunjie Zhu,Xingtong Ge,Fangyu Lin,Yi Zhang,Zhening Liu,Mengfei Li,Yumeng Zhang,Guanglu Song,Yu Liu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Omni-LiveAvatar是首个支持分钟级、实时流式音视频联合生成的数字人框架，通过渐进式自回归蒸馏、同步音视频长短时记忆和分层滚动提示规划，在单张NVIDIA H200 GPU上相较于其教师模型LTX-2实现了33倍生成加速，同时超越了各加速基线在视觉质量、音频质量、跨模态同步和人类保真度上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频联合生成模型依赖双向注意力机制和多步去噪过程，且只能生成短视频片段，无法满足长时间实时交互的需求，亟需一个能够生成分钟级、实时流式音视频数字人的框架。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1)渐进式自回归蒸馏管道，将大型双向联合音视频扩散模型转换为少步自回归生成器，无需辅助稳定机制；(2)同步音视频长短期记忆模块，在有限记忆预算下保持全局一致性；(3)分层滚动提示规划策略，支持连贯的语义演化和平滑的提示词切换。

Result: 在单张NVIDIA H200 GPU上实现33倍于教师模型LTX-2的生成加速；在视觉质量、音频质量、跨模态同步和人类保真度等指标上均优于加速基线方法；能够实时生成高质量且同步的分钟级音视频数字人。

Conclusion: Omni-LiveAvatar首次实现了分钟级实时流式音视频联合生成，验证了通过蒸馏、记忆管理和提示规划相结合可以克服现有模型的实时性和时长限制，为沉浸式交互数字人生成奠定了新基础。

Abstract: Joint audio-video generative models serve as foundation for immersive and interactive digital-human generation. Nevertheless, most existing models rely on bidirectional attention and multi-step denoising and can generate only short clips, making them unsuitable for real-time interaction over extended durations. We present Omni-LiveAvatar, the first framework for minute-level, real-time streaming joint audio-video avatar generation. Specifically, we propose (1) a progressive autoregressive distillation pipeline that transfers a large bidirectional joint audio-video diffusion model into a few-step autoregressive generator without auxiliary stabilization mechanisms; (2) a synchronized audio-video long-short-term memory that preserves global consistency under a bounded memory budget; and (3) a hierarchical rolling prompt planning strategy that enables coherent semantic evolution and seamless prompt transitions. Extensive experiments show that Omni-LiveAvatar generates high-quality, synchronized minute-level avatars in real time. In terms of speed, it achieves a 33$\times$ generation speedup over its teacher, LTX-2, on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU; in terms of generation quality, it outperforms accelerated baselines across visual quality, audio quality, cross-modal synchronization, and human fidelity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Aoko955/Omni-LiveAvatar.

</details>


### [200] [AlignFace: Human-Aligned Face Similarity Metric with Interpretable Concept Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14130)
*Ying Huang,Wencan Zhang,Brian Y. Lim*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AlignFace，一种融合认知心理学原理的可解释人脸相似度度量方法，通过引入FACETS数据集和ante-hoc建模（VLM编码、门控交叉注意力、概念瓶颈模型和神经GAM），在人类亚群体感知对齐上显著优于现有基线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉感知相似度度量仅依赖隐式且虚假的关系，假设统一观察者，缺乏认知对齐，无法准确反映不同人群对面部内容感知的差异，导致生成模型调试的误导性指导。

Method: 提出FACETS数据集和AlignFace指标。AlignFace利用视觉语言模型（VLM）编码成对面部图像和基于文本的属性，采用门控交叉注意力（CA）提取属性特定的面部差异表征，通过概念瓶颈建模（CBM）以可解释的面部属性约束推理，并用神经广义加性模型（GAM）建模属性的非线性影响。

Result: 实验表明，AlignFace在人类亚群体感知对齐方面显著优于包括最新无领域学习感知指标在内的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过桥接学习表征与人类认知过程，AlignFace为面部图像提供更透明、更对齐的感知评估度量，支持生成模型的可解释调试与更公平的评估。

Abstract: Computer vision models for generated facial content, such as face editing and privacy protection, increasingly affect people, requiring similarity metrics that serve as faithful proxies for human perception. While perceptual evaluation has progressed from signal-based heuristics to representation-based metrics, current approaches are limited to behavioral modeling without cognitive alignment. They rely on implicit and spurious relations while assuming a universal observer, failing to account for inherent variations across diverse human populations. This leads to inaccurate evaluative models of stakeholders and misleading guidance for generative model debugging. Rather than treating perception as a black box, we leverage scientific findings from cognitive psychology of human face similarity perception: dependence on facial featural and configural attributes, nonlinear psychophysical response scaling, and own-group biases. We introduce the FACETS dataset and propose AlignFace, an interpretable, human-aligned, face similarity metric that encodes these cognitive principles through ante-hoc modeling. It employs visual-language modeling (VLM) to encode paired face images and text-based attributes, gated cross-attention (CA) to extract attribute-specific facial difference representations, concept bottleneck modeling (CBM) to constrain reasoning via interpretable face attributes, and neural generalized additive model (GAM) to model their nonlinear influence. Experiments found AlignFace significantly improves alignment with human subpopulation perceptions compared to baseline metrics, including recent domain-free learned perceptual metrics. By bridging learned representations and human cognitive processes, this work enables more transparent and aligned perceptual evaluation metrics for face images.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Dependence-Informed Sparse Neural Architecture for Stock Return Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14323)
*Hongyu Lin,Yulin Chen,Yuanrong Wang,Antonio Briola,Tomaso Aste*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最大过滤团森林（MFCF）映射同调神经网络（HNN）的股票收益预测方法，用图形学意义上的最大团大小K作为唯一架构控制参数，替代传统神经网络中难以解释的深度和宽度选择。在1987至2016年美国股票超额收益预测中，HNN在准确性和排序能力上与三层基准网络相当甚至更优，且参数量比全连接网络减少约80倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络在股票收益预测中需要选择深度和隐藏层宽度，但这些超参数难以与金融解释建立联系，导致模型设计缺乏可解释性和理论指导。

Method: 首先利用最大过滤团森林（MFCF）估计公司特征之间的依赖关系，然后将团结构映射为同调神经网络（HNN）的架构。最大团大小K是唯一控制架构复杂度的参数，过滤图在训练前确定了网络的深度、层宽和稀疏连接。论文测试了两种HNN变体。

Result: HNN模型在池化预测准确性上与三层隐藏层基准模型相当，在横截面排序上更准确，参数数量约为具有相同诱导层宽的全连接网络的1/80。两种结构消融实验表明稀疏连接和特征分组的估计都有助于排序优势，且经多重检验校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: HNN提供了一种实用且可解释的方式，将公司特征间的估计依赖关系融入神经网络架构设计中，在不损失预测性能的前提下大幅降低参数复杂度。

Abstract: Using neural networks for stock return prediction typically requires choices about depth and hidden-layer width that are difficult to connect to financial interpretation. We study an alternative: estimate dependence among firm characteristics with a Maximally Filtered Clique Forest (MFCF), then map its clique structure to a Homological Neural Network (HNN). The MFCF maximum clique size K is the only parameter controlling architectural complexity, and it has a clear graphical meaning: it bounds the number of characteristics in each maximal clique and hence the highest interaction order the network can represent. The filtered graph then fixes the neural network's depth, layer widths, and sparse connections before training, in place of a separately chosen depth and width sequence. We apply two HNN variants to annual out-of-sample forecasts of U.S. stock excess returns from 1987 to 2016 using 94 firm characteristics. The HNN models match a three-hidden-layer benchmark on pooled predictive accuracy, rank the cross-section more accurately, and use roughly 80 times fewer parameters than a fully connected network with the same induced layer widths. Two structural ablations indicate that both the sparse connectivity and the estimated grouping of characteristics contribute to the ranking advantage, and both effects remain significant after correcting for multiple testing. These findings show that HNNs offer a practical and interpretable way to incorporate estimated dependence among firm characteristics into neural architecture design.

</details>
